It downloads\n`https://www.ecb.europa.eu/stats/eurofxref/eurofxref-hist.zip`, extracts the member\n`eurofxref-hist.csv`, and loads that text exactly as `fxrates load` does.\n\n## Trigger\n\nThe command line: `./fxrates fetch`. It takes no arguments; a wrong argument count is a usage error\n(exit 2) handled by the shared validation.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. Call `fxrates_app.fetch.download_archive(url)` for the ECB URL. It returns the archive bytes and\n   uses `urllib` with a builder-chosen timeout. It is the only function in the application that\n   opens a network connection.\n2. The handler must call it through the module attribute (`fxrates_app.fetch.download_archive(...)`),\n   never through a name imported into another module, so tests replace the single seam.\n3. Open the bytes as a zip archive and read the member `eurofxref-hist.csv`.\n4. Pass its text through the same parse, validate, and transactional store path used by `load`\n   (`fxrates_app.ecb.parse_history`, then one transaction of upserts).\n5. Print `dates,rates` then `<dates>,<rates>`, as `load` does, and exit 0.\n\n## Exit codes\n\n| Condition | Exit | Stdout | Stderr | Stored |\n|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|\n| Success | 0 | `dates,rates` then counts | diagnostics only | the rates |\n| Download fails or times out | 1 | empty | one line | nothing |\n| Bytes are not a zip archive | 1 | empty | one line | nothing |\n| Archive lacks `eurofxref-hist.csv` | 1 | empty | one line | nothing |\n| Member is a rejected file | 3 | empty | one line | nothing |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md and reached through the load path.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC fetch-loads-archive ===\nIntent: With the download stubbed to return a zip holding the CSV, fetch exits 0, reports the date and rate counts, and stores each published rate text exactly.\nRequires: executable=python3; Port validation (integer, 1 to 65535, otherwise exit 2) is owned by\nthe usage-error story and runs before any socket is bound.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. Register the `serve` handler with the dispatcher.\n2. Run uvicorn on `app_server.main:app` bound to `127.0.0.1` at the port, default `8000`, in the\n   foreground. Nothing is required on standard output.\n3. On SIGTERM stop cleanly and exit 0.\n4. If the port is already bound, exit 1 with one stderr line and answer no requests.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Status | Body |\n|-------|--------|------|\n| `GET /health` | 200 | `{\"status\": \"ok\"}` |\n| `GET /openapi.json` | 200 | the FastAPI OpenAPI description |\n| `GET /docs` | 200 | the interactive reference |\n\nThe OpenAPI and docs endpoints stay enabled. `/health` does not touch the database.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md and `/health` reaches no store.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC serve-health-route ===\nIntent: The application answers GET /health with status 200 and the exact status body, and its OpenAPI description and interactive docs are served with status 200.\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nclient = web_client()\nresponse = client.get(\"/health\")\nassert response.status_code == 200\nassert response.json() == {\"status\": \"ok\"}\n\nspec = client.get(\"/openapi.json\")\nassert spec.status_code == 200\nassert \"/health\" in spec.json()[\"paths\"]\n\ndocs = client.get(\"/docs\")\nassert docs.status_code == 200\n=== END AC serve-health-route ===\n\n=== AC serve-live-sigterm ===\nIntent: The serve command listens on 127.0.0.1 at the requested port, answers /health within a bounded wait, and exits 0 when it receives SIGTERM.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=uvicorn; scope=test\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport signal\nimport socket\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\nimport time\nimport urllib.request\n\ns = socket.socket()\ns.bind((\"127.0.0.1\", 0))\nport = s.getsockname()[1]\ns.close()\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nenv = {**os.environ, \"DATABASE_URL\": os.path.join(work, \"serve.db\")}\nproc = subprocess.Popen(\n    [os.path.abspath(\"fxrates\"), \"serve\", \"--port\", str(port)],\n    stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=env,\n)\ntry:\n    deadline = time.time() + 30\n    body = None\n    while time.time() < deadline:\n        if proc.poll() is not None:\n            break\n        try:\n            with urllib.request.urlopen(f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/health\", timeout=2) as r:\n                assert r.status == 200\n                body = json.loads(r.read())\n            break\n        except OSError:\n            time.sleep(0.25)\n    assert proc.poll() is None, \"server exited before answering /health\"\n    assert body == {\"status\": \"ok\"}\n    proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)\n    rc = proc.wait(timeout=20)\n    assert rc == 0\nfinally:\n    if proc.poll() is None:\n        proc.kill()\n    out, err = proc.communicate()\n    print(out.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\"))\n    print(err.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\"))\n=== END AC serve-live-sigterm ===\n\n=== AC serve-port-in-use ===\nIntent: When the requested port is already bound, serve exits 1 with empty stdout and one stderr line instead of serving.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=uvicorn; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport socket\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\n\nblocker = socket.socket()\nblocker.bind((\"127.0.0.1\", 0))\nblocker.listen(1)\nport = blocker.getsockname()[1]\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nenv = {**os.environ, \"DATABASE_URL\": os.path.join(work, \"busy.db\")}\nproc = subprocess.Popen(\n    [os.path.abspath(\"fxrates\"), \"serve\", \"--port\", str(port)],\n    stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=env,\n)\ntry:\n    out, err = proc.communicate(timeout=30)\n    print(out.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\"))\n    print(err.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\"))\n    assert proc.returncode == 1\n    assert out == b\"\"\n    assert len(err.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\").strip().splitlines()) == 1\nfinally:\n    if proc.poll() is None:\n        proc.kill()\n        proc.communicate()\n    blocker.close()\n=== END AC serve-port-in-use ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The server binds 127.0.0.1 only and has no authentication; it serves public ECB reference rates.\n- Standard error is diagnostics only and is never compared.\n- `serve` stops cleanly on SIGTERM and exits 0.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Stats.md ===\n# FEATURE: Stats\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | `GET /stats` reports the number of stored dates and rates and the first and last publication dates. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-ServeHealth.md, FEATURE-LoadStore.md |\n| Provides | GET /stats |\n| Consumes | rates.list_all_keys, app_server.create_app |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive a summary of what the store holds.\n\n## Route\n\n`GET /stats` returns status 200 with `{\"dates\": <n>, \"rates\": <n>, \"first\": <date>, \"last\": <date>}`.\n\n- `dates` is the number of distinct publication dates with a stored rate.\n- `rates` is the number of stored rates.\n- `first` and `last` are the earliest and latest stored dates, and are `null` when nothing is stored.\n- `dates` and `rates` are JSON integers.\n\nEvery value is computed from `rates.list_all_keys` called without `since`; the service counts and\ntakes the first and last date. Nothing is hardcoded.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-004` — read: list every stored date and currency - supplies: — - returns: `date`, `currency`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC stats-reflects-store ===\nIntent: The stats body equals the counts, first date and last date derived from the stored keys read back through the repository, and includes the rows the criterion seeded.\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Fetch.md ===\n# FEATURE: Fetch\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | `fxrates fetch` downloads the ECB history archive, extracts | fxrates load, fxrates_app.ecb.parse_history |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`fxrates fetch` obtains the ECB euro reference rate history without a file argument. It downloads\n`https://www.ecb.europa.eu/stats/eurofxref/eurofxref-hist.zip`, extracts the member\n`eurofxref-hist.csv`, and loads that text exactly as `fxrates load` does.\n\n## Trigger\n\nThe command line: `./fxrates fetch`. It takes no arguments; a wrong argument count is a usage error\n(exit 2) handled by the shared validation.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. Call `fxrates_app.fetch.download_archive(url)` for the ECB URL. It returns the archive bytes and\n   uses `urllib` with a builder-chosen timeout. It is the only function in the application that\n   opens a network connection.\n2. The handler must call it through the module attribute (`fxrates_app.fetch.download_archive(...)`),\n   never through a name imported into another module, so tests replace the single seam.\n3. Open the bytes as a zip archive and read the member `eurofxref-hist.csv`.\n4. Pass its text through the same parse, validate, and transactional store path used by `load`\n   (`fxrates_app.ecb.parse_history`, then one transaction of upserts).\n5. Print `dates,rates` then `<dates>,<rates>`, as `load` does, and exit 0.\n\n## Exit codes\n\n| Condition | Exit | Stdout | Stderr | Stored |\n|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|\n| Success | 0 | `dates,rates` then counts | diagnostics only | the rates |\n| Download fails or times out | 1 | empty | one line | nothing |\n| Bytes are not a zip archive | 1 | empty | one line | nothing |\n| Archive lacks `eurofxref-hist.csv` | 1 | empty | one line | nothing |\n| Member is a rejected file | 3 | empty | one line | nothing |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md and reached through the load path.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC fetch-loads-archive ===\nIntent: With the download stubbed to return a zip holding the CSV, fetch exits 0, reports the date and rate counts, and stores each published rate text exactly.\nRequires: executable=python3; Port validation (integer, 1 to 65535, otherwise exit 2) is owned by\nthe usage-error story and runs before any socket is bound.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. Register the `serve` handler with the dispatcher.\n2. Run uvicorn on `app_server.main:app` bound to `127.0.0.1` at the port, default `8000`, in the\n   foreground. Nothing is required on standard output.\n3. On SIGTERM stop cleanly and exit 0.\n4. If the port is already bound, exit 1 with one stderr line and answer no requests.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Status | Body |\n|-------|--------|------|\n| `GET /health` | 200 | `{\"status\": \"ok\"}` |\n| `GET /openapi.json` | 200 | the FastAPI OpenAPI description |\n| `GET /docs` | 200 | the interactive reference |\n\nThe OpenAPI and docs endpoints stay enabled. `/health` does not touch the database.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md and `/health` reaches no store.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC serve-health-route ===\nIntent: The application answers GET /health with status 200 and the exact status body, and its OpenAPI description and interactive docs are served with status 200.\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\n\nclient = web_client()\nresponse = client.get(\"/health\")\nassert response.status_code == 200\nassert response.json() == {\"status\": \"ok\"}\n\nspec = client.get(\"/openapi.json\")\nassert spec.status_code == 200\nassert \"/health\" in spec.json()[\"paths\"]\n\ndocs = client.get(\"/docs\")\nassert docs.status_code == 200\n=== END AC serve-health-route ===\n\n=== AC serve-live-sigterm ===\nIntent: The serve command listens on 127.0.0.1 at the requested port, answers /health within a bounded wait, and exits 0 when it receives SIGTERM.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=uvicorn; scope=test\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport signal\nimport socket\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\nimport time\nimport urllib.request\n\ns = socket.socket()\ns.bind((\"127.0.0.1\", 0))\nport = s.getsockname()[1]\ns.close()\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nenv = {**os.environ, \"DATABASE_URL\": os.path.join(work, \"serve.db\")}\nproc = subprocess.Popen(\n    [os.path.abspath(\"fxrates\"), \"serve\", \"--port\", str(port)],\n    stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=env,\n)\ntry:\n    deadline = time.time() + 30\n    body = None\n    while time.time() < deadline:\n        if proc.poll() is not None:\n            break\n        try:\n            with urllib.request.urlopen(f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/health\", timeout=2) as r:\n                assert r.status == 200\n                body = json.loads(r.read())\n            break\n        except OSError:\n            time.sleep(0.25)\n    assert proc.poll() is None, \"server exited before answering /health\"\n    assert body == {\"status\": \"ok\"}\n    proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)\n    rc = proc.wait(timeout=20)\n    assert rc == 0\nfinally:\n    if proc.poll() is None:\n        proc.kill()\n    out, err = proc.communicate()\n    print(out.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\"))\n    print(err.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\"))\n=== END AC serve-live-sigterm ===\n\n=== AC serve-port-in-use ===\nIntent: When the requested port is already bound, serve exits 1 with empty stdout and one stderr line instead of serving.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=uvicorn; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport socket\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\n\nblocker = socket.socket()\nblocker.bind((\"127.0.0.1\", 0))\nblocker.listen(1)\nport = blocker.getsockname()[1]\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nenv = {**os.environ, \"DATABASE_URL\": os.path.join(work, \"busy.db\")}\nproc = subprocess.Popen(\n    [os.path.abspath(\"fxrates\"), \"serve\", \"--port\", str(port)],\n    stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=env,\n)\ntry:\n    out, err = proc.communicate(timeout=30)\n    print(out.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\"))\n    print(err.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\"))\n    assert proc.returncode == 1\n    assert out == b\"\"\n    assert len(err.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\").strip().splitlines()) == 1\nfinally:\n    if proc.poll() is None:\n        proc.kill()\n        proc.communicate()\n    blocker.close()\n=== END AC serve-port-in-use ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The server binds 127.0.0.1 only and has no authentication; it serves public ECB reference rates.\n- Standard error is diagnostics only and is never compared.\n- `serve` stops cleanly on SIGTERM and exits 0.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Stats.md ===\n# FEATURE: Stats\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | `GET /stats` reports the number of stored dates and rates and the first and last publication dates. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-ServeHealth.md, FEATURE-LoadStore.md |\n| Provides | GET /stats |\n| Consumes | rates.list_all_keys, app_server.create_app |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive a summary of what the store holds.\n\n## Route\n\n`GET /stats` returns status 200 with `{\"dates\": <n>, \"rates\": <n>, \"first\": <date>, \"last\": <date>}`.\n\n- `dates` is the number of distinct publication dates with a stored rate.\n- `rates` is the number of stored rates.\n- `first` and `last` are the earliest and latest stored dates, and are `null` when nothing is stored.\n- `dates` and `rates` are JSON integers.\n\nEvery value is computed from `rates.list_all_keys` called without `since`; the service counts and\ntakes the first and last date. Nothing is hardcoded.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-004` — read: list every stored date and currency - supplies: — - returns: `date`, `currency`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC stats-reflects-store ===\nIntent: The stats body equals the counts, first date and last date derived from the stored keys read back through the repository, and includes the rows the criterion seeded.\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nd1 = \"1900-09-01\"\nd2 = \"1900-09-02\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=d1, currency=\"ZZA\", rate=\"1.1\")\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=d1, currency=\"ZZB\", rate=\"2.2\")\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=d2, currency=\"ZZA\", rate=\"1.2\")\n    keys = db.rates.list_all_keys()\n    dates = sorted({k.date for k in keys})\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(\"/stats\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n    assert body[\"dates\"] == len(dates)\n    assert body[\"rates\"] == len(keys)\n    assert body[\"first\"] == dates[0]\n    assert body[\"last\"] == dates[-1]\n    assert isinstance(body[\"dates\"], int)\n    assert isinstance(body[\"rates\"], int)\n    assert body[\"first\"] <= d1\n    assert body[\"last\"] >= d2\n=== END AC stats-reflects-store ===\n\n=== AC stats-body-shape ===\nIntent: The stats body carries exactly the four declared keys, and first and last are null exactly when no rate is stored.\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; The ECB publishes on working days only, so a request\nfor any date uses the latest publication on or before it.\n\n## Effective date\n\n`fxrates_app.rates.effective_date(d)` returns the latest publication date on or before `d`, or\n`None` when no such date exists. It is computed with `rates.list_on_or_before(as_of=d)`; the first\nrow's date is the effective date. Other stories (`/convert`, `/history`, `/rates/latest`) reuse it.\n\n## Route\n\n`GET /rates/{date}`:\n\n| Condition | Status | Body |\n|-----------|--------|------|\n| `date` is a real calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD` with an effective date | 200 | `{\"date\": <effective>, \"requested\": <date>, \"base\": \"EUR\", \"rates\": {<code>: <stored text>, ...}}` |\n| `date` is a real date with no publication on or before it | 404 | any |\n| `date` is malformed or not a real date (for example `2025-13-01`) | 422 | any |\n\n- `rates` holds every currency with a rate on the effective date, as the stored text in a JSON\n  string, and has no `EUR` entry.\n- The date is validated through FastAPI request validation, which produces 422.\n- Static routes such as `/rates/latest` are registered before this dynamic route so `latest` is not\n  parsed as a date.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-003` — read: list publication dates on or before a date - supplies: `as_of` - returns: `date`\n- `FEATURE-RATE-002` — read: list every rate published on one date - supplies: `date` - returns: `date`, `currency`, `rate`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC rates-by-date-exact ===\nIntent: A request for a publication date returns that date as both date and requested, base EUR, and each stored rate text exactly as supplied.\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nd = \"1900-10-01\"\nra = \"1.50\"\nrb = \"2.250\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=\"ZZA\", rate=ra)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=d, currency=\"ZZB\", rate=rb)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(f\"/rates/{d}\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n    assert body[\"date\"] == d\n    assert body[\"requested\"] == d\n    assert body[\"base\"] == \"EUR\"\n    assert body[\"rates\"][\"ZZA\"] == ra\n    assert body[\"rates\"][\"ZZB\"] == rb\n    assert \"EUR\" not in body[\"rates\"]\n    stored = {r.currency: r.rate for r in db.rates.list_for_date(date=d)}\n    assert body[\"rates\"] == stored\n=== END AC rates-by-date-exact ===\n\n=== AC rates-by-date-effective ===\nIntent: A request between two publications resolves to the earlier publication, keeps the requested date, and carries that earlier date's rates rather than the later ones.\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nearly = \"1900-10-01\"\nlate = \"1900-10-05\"\nbetween = \"1900-10-03\"\nr_early = \"1.50\"\nr_late = \"1.6\"\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=early, currency=\"ZZA\", rate=r_early)\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=late, currency=\"ZZA\", rate=r_late)\n    client = web_client()\n    response = client.get(f\"/rates/{between}\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    body = response.json()\n    assert body[\"date\"] == early\n    assert body[\"requested\"] == between\n    assert body[\"rates\"][\"ZZA\"] == r_early\n=== END AC rates-by-date-effective ===\n\n=== AC rates-effective-date-function ===\nIntent: effective_date returns the latest publication date on or before its argument and None when none exists.\nRequires: executable=python3;
